intdb {
	enum DSP_MODE {
		MULT9X9,
		MULT9X9ADDSUB,
		MULT9X9ADDSUBSUM,
		MULT18X18,
		MULT18X18MAC,
		MULT18X18ADDSUB,
		MULT18X18ADDSUBSUM,
		MULT36X36,
	}

	enum DSP_MUX_P3 {
		P,
		SRO,
	}

	bel_class SLICE {
		input A[2];
		input B[2];
		input C[2];
		input D[2];
		input M[2];
		input FXA;
		input FXB;
		input CLK;
		input LSR;
		input CE;
		output F[2];
		output Q[2];
		output OFX[2];
	}

	bel_class EBR {
		input CLKA;
		input CLKB;
		input RSTA;
		input RSTB;
		input CEA;
		input CEB;
		input WEA;
		input WEB;
		input MORCLKA;
		input MORCLKB;
		input CSA[3];
		input CSB[3];
		input ADA[14];
		input ADB[14];
		input ADB3W;
		input BLENABLE;
		input BHENABLE;
		input DIA[18];
		input DIB[18];
		output DOA[18];
		output DOB[18];
	}

	bel_class DSP {
		input CLK[4];

				mux IMUX_CLK[1] = X0_W[0] | X0_W[1] | X0_E[0] | X0_E[1] | X2_W1[4] | X2_W2[4] | X2_E1[4] | X2_E2[4] | X2_S1[4] | X2_S2[4] | X2_N1[4] | X2_N2[4] | PCLK[0] | PCLK[1] | PCLK[2] | PCLK[3] | PCLK[4] | PCLK[5] | PCLK[6] | PCLK[7] | RSCLK[0] | RSCLK[1] | RSCLK[2] | RSCLK[3];

		tile_class PLL_S {
			cell CELL[0];
			cell CELL[1];
			cell IO_CLKI;
			cell IO_CLKFB;

			switchbox BEL_INT {
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKI = CELL[0].IMUX_A[0] | CELL[0].IMUX_B[0] | CELL[0].IMUX_CLK[0] | IO_CLKI.OUT_IO_PADDI[0];
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKFB = CELL[0].ECLK[0] | CELL[0].IMUX_CLK[1] | CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKINTFB | IO_CLKFB.OUT_IO_PADDI[0];
			}

			bel PLL[0] {
				input CLKI = CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKI;
				input CLKFB = CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKFB;
				input RESETM = CELL[0].IMUX_LSR[1];
				input RESETK = CELL[0].IMUX_CE[0];
				input CNTRST = CELL[0].IMUX_LSR[0];
				input PWD = CELL[0].IMUX_A[4];
				input DPAMODE = CELL[0].IMUX_A[3];
				input DRPAI[0] = CELL[0].IMUX_B[1];
				input DRPAI[1] = CELL[0].IMUX_D[1];
				input DRPAI[2] = CELL[0].IMUX_B[2];
				input DRPAI[3] = CELL[0].IMUX_D[2];
				input DFPAI[0] = CELL[0].IMUX_A[1];
				input DFPAI[1] = CELL[0].IMUX_C[1];
				input DFPAI[2] = CELL[0].IMUX_A[2];
				input DFPAI[3] = CELL[0].IMUX_C[2];
				input TCLKI = CELL[0].IMUX_B[4];
				output CLKOP = CELL[0].OUT_F[5], CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOP;
				output CLKOS = CELL[0].OUT_F[6], CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOS;
				output CLKOK = CELL[0].OUT_F[2], CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOK;
				output CLKOK2 = CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOK2, CELL[1].OUT_Q[5];
				output CLKINTFB = CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKINTFB;
				output LOCK = CELL[0].OUT_Q[0];
				output DNLOCK = CELL[0].OUT_Q[3];
				output UPLOCK = CELL[0].OUT_Q[2];
				output DRPAO[0] = CELL[0].OUT_F[0];
				output DRPAO[1] = CELL[0].OUT_F[1];
				output DRPAO[2] = CELL[0].OUT_Q[4];
				output DRPAO[3] = CELL[0].OUT_F[3];
				output DFPAO[0] = CELL[0].OUT_F[4];
				output DFPAO[1] = CELL[0].OUT_Q[5];
				output DFPAO[2] = CELL[1].OUT_F[6];
				output DFPAO[3] = CELL[0].OUT_F[7];
				output TESTOUT = CELL[0].OUT_Q[1];
			}

				input DFPAI[0] = CELL[0].IMUX_A[1];

				input DFPAI[2] = CELL[0].IMUX_A[2];
				input DFPAI[3] = CELL[0].IMUX_C[2];
				input TCLKI = CELL[0].IMUX_B[4];
				output CLKOP = CELL[0].OUT_F[5], CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOP;
				output CLKOS = CELL[0].OUT_F[6], CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOS;
				output CLKOK = CELL[0].OUT_F[2], CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOK;
				output CLKOK2 = CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOK2, CELL[1].OUT_Q[5];
				output CLKINTFB = CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKINTFB;
				output LOCK = CELL[0].OUT_Q[0];
				output DNLOCK = CELL[0].OUT_Q[3];
				output UPLOCK = CELL[0].OUT_Q[2];
				output DRPAO[0] = CELL[0].OUT_F[0];
				output DRPAO[1] = CELL[0].OUT_F[1];
				output DRPAO[2] = CELL[0].OUT_Q[4];
				output DRPAO[3] = CELL[0].OUT_F[3];
				output DFPAO[0] = CELL[0].OUT_F[4];
				output DFPAO[1] = CELL[0].OUT_Q[5];
				output DFPAO[2] = CELL[1].OUT_F[6];
				output DFPAO[3] = CELL[0].OUT_F[7];
				output TESTOUT = CELL[0].OUT_Q[1];
			}

	connector_slot RSCLK_W {
		opposite RSCLK_E;

		connector_class PASS_SW {
			pass RSCLK_H[0] = RSCLK_H[1];
			pass RSCLK_H[1] = RSCLK_H[2];
			pass RSCLK_H[2] = RSCLK_H[3];
			pass RSCLK_H[3] = RSCLK_H[0];
			pass RSCLK_H[4] = RSCLK_H[5];
			pass RSCLK_H[5] = RSCLK_H[6];
			pass RSCLK_H[6] = RSCLK_H[7];
			pass RSCLK_H[7] = RSCLK_H[4];
		}
	}

	connector_slot RSCLK_E {
		opposite RSCLK_W;

		connector_class PASS_SE {
		}
	}
}
